- **cow-debug-investigator**: Use to diagnose one bug domain read-only and return an implementation-ready root-cause contract. Reproduces, gathers evidence, traces flow, tests bounded hypotheses, and may run tests/builds/read-only git via Bash — but never edits tracked files, installs deps, creates tracked instrumentation, commits, or returns a patch. Returns REQUIRES_REROUTE when tracked instrumentation is needed; BLOCKED when it cannot reproduce. ([gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow](https://github.com/gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/cow-debug-investigator.md))
- **cow-implementer**: Use to implement one explicitly bounded unit from a controller-generated task brief. Edits only the brief's ALLOWED_PATHS, runs fresh verification, self-reviews, and writes an ignored report artifact. Never commits, never updates workflow state, never marks the unit complete, never broadens scope, never spawns another agent. Returns at most 8 lines; BLOCKED_INPUT when inputs are missing or contradictory. ([gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow](https://github.com/gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/cow-implementer.md))
- **cow-repo-investigator**: Use to draft a bounded semantic repository profile from a controller-supplied repo-snapshot.json, the profile contract, and a few named files. Read-only, no shell. Returns a profile-draft envelope tagging each claim verified/inferred/unknown for the controller to finalize. Never edits, commits, runs commands, writes the profile, or explores broadly; returns BLOCKED_INPUT when required inputs are missing. ([gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow](https://github.com/gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/cow-repo-investigator.md))
- **cow-reviewer**: Independently review a unit, targeted re-review, or whole-work package against its brief — read-only, never the implementer. Returns a compact JSON report (schema v1): spec/quality/overall verdicts with each finding classified by causality (introduced/worsened/pre-existing/uncertain); only introduced/worsened Critical/Important findings block. Never writes, edits, commits, or runs shell; returns BLOCKED_INPUT when inputs missing. ([gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow](https://github.com/gencberke/cost-oriented-agentic-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/cow-reviewer.md))

([cicorias/agent-design-crc-process](https://github.com/cicorias/agent-design-crc-process/blob/HEAD/agents/crc-card.md))
- **crc-nominator**: Extracts candidate classes and responsibilities from scenarios, requirements, glossaries, or existing code for a CRC session. Use during the nominate phase, or whenever a long document needs to be reduced to a triage list of design candidates. ([cicorias/agent-design-crc-process](https://github.com/cicorias/agent-design-crc-process/blob/HEAD/agents/crc-nominator.md))
- **crc-scout**: Reads requirements documents, transcripts, tickets, wikis, or an existing codebase and extracts candidate domain purpose, boundary, actors, and vocabulary before any design starts. Use during domain discovery when the user hands over material rather than answering questions, so the interrogation can start from what the documents already say instead of asking about it. ([cicorias/agent-design-crc-process](https://github.com/cicorias/agent-design-crc-process/blob/HEAD/agents/crc-scout.md))
- **crc-scribe**: Commits a CRC session to disk — writes deck.json against the schema, updates the session log, runs validation, and generates Mermaid sequence diagrams from scenario traces. Use at the end of a session, after each scenario walkthrough, or whenever the deck needs to be persisted. ([cicorias/agent-design-crc-process](https://github.com/cicorias/agent-design-crc-process/blob/HEAD/agents/crc-scribe.md))
- **crc-skeptic**: Critiques a CRC card deck for god classes, anemic cards, feature envy, collaborator cycles, orphans, and load-bearing assumptions. Use after each scenario walkthrough, or when reviewing an object model someone else designed. ([cicorias/agent-design-crc-process](https://github.com/cicorias/agent-design-crc-process/blob/HEAD/agents/crc-skeptic.md))

- **crim-defesa**: Despache quando houver defesa criminal a redigir na fase de conhecimento — resposta à acusação, alegações finais, memoriais, revogação de preventiva, liberdade provisória — ou quando for preciso escolher entre teses de defesa em crime que não seja doloso contra a vida ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-defesa.md))
- **crim-execucao**: Despache quando o cliente já estiver cumprindo pena — progressão de regime, livramento condicional, remição, saída temporária, prisão domiciliar, indulto, unificação de penas ou defesa em procedimento de falta grave ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-execucao.md))
- **crim-juri**: Despache quando o caso envolver crime doloso contra a vida — homicídio, infanticídio, aborto, induzimento ao suicídio, consumados ou tentados — em qualquer fase do rito escalonado, incluindo pronúncia, desclassificação, quesitação e preparação de plenário ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-juri.md))
- **crim-recursos**: Despache quando houver decisão criminal a impugnar ou liberdade urgente a buscar — habeas corpus, relaxamento, apelação, RESE, agravo em execução, recurso especial ou extraordinário, embargos, revisão criminal ([rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia](https://github.com/rogeriotravagin/escritorio-ia/blob/HEAD/agents/crim-recursos.md))

- **crisis-sentinel**: Crisis pattern detector. Monitors for signal storms, mandate pileups, inbox backlogs, hook slowdowns, and source/runtime divergence. - **crisis-steward**: Crisis lifecycle coordinator. CENTROID: crisis lifecycle coordination across detection → containment → dissonance absorption → diagnosis → restoration → resolution → learning → prevention IS: - escalation posture coordinator (reads crisis-sentinel signals; authorizes containment when warranted) - containment-authorization gate (containment-operator does not act without Crisis Steward authorization) - restoration… ([prompted365/context-grapple-gun](https://github.com/prompted365/context-grapple-gun/blob/HEAD/cgg-runtime/agents/crisis-steward.md) · ★3)

- **criteria-refresher**: Read-only drift checker for the reviewers' own criteria files — the skill reviewer's and the subagent reviewer's checklists, and the shared prompt criteria. Use when a maintainer sets out to reconcile the criteria, typically because a review reported them as old. Not part of a review — no review step spawns this, and it never reads an artifact under review. ([VeigaPunk/benxshitter](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/benxshitter/blob/HEAD/marketplace/plugins/xbrd-gdsp-fknpft/templates/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: DS4CC critic — Godspeed specialist. ([VeigaPunk/benxshitter](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/benxshitter/blob/HEAD/marketplace/plugins/the-musketeer/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: DS4CC critic ([VeigaPunk/benxshitter](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/benxshitter/blob/HEAD/marketplace/plugins/ds4cc/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: Use to challenge a plan, refactor, test strategy, or large change for hidden assumptions, weak boundaries, or overengineering. ([xxziiko/claude-code-style-kit](https://github.com/xxziiko/claude-code-style-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: 反証専任の批判者 (devil's advocate)。原因の断定・設計判断・「〜が本命」という結論を、逆の前提で崩しにかかる。原因特定・仮説採択・docs への「原因特定済み」記載の前に必ず 1 体起こす (CLAUDE.md 作業慣例)。読み取り専用。 ([YamamotoSoya/reRoBot](https://github.com/YamamotoSoya/reRoBot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: Critical reviewer (비평 연구원 엄서윤, "Reviewer 2"). Reviews ideas, pre-registered plans, results (auditing code, run logs and `lab.py audit` output, not just the report), campaigns, and drafts; judges brainstorm candidates pairwise. Writes a REV note with a verdict. Use only when 교수님 asks for a strict review (/ai-lab:review), and to judge brainstorm candidates pairwise. ([yhlee52/ai_sci_cowork](https://github.com/yhlee52/ai_sci_cowork/blob/HEAD/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: 답변(Answer)의 근거 체인 독립 비판 전문가. answer-formulator 산출물에 대해 4축(Grounding Validity / Support Strength / Counter-Evidence / Verifiability) 각 0~5 점수를 부여하고, 각 Evidence가 Grounding≥3 AND Support≥3 AND Verifiability≥3 통과해야 한다. weak Evidence는 FLAGS_WEAK, Counter-Evidence는 Paper/Claim id로 고정. "답변 비판", "근거 검증", "counter-evidence", "grounding 검토" 관련 요청 시 호출된다. ([youai058/research_hub](https://github.com/youai058/research_hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: Delegate to this subagent for gate G20 SELF_EVAL, after G17-G19 pass and before the human is bothered. Give it only rendered evidence, never the composition source, so its judgment isn't colored by having built the thing. ([yousuf7474/demo-studio](https://github.com/yousuf7474/demo-studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic.md))
- **critic**: Independent critical pass over {source, spec, ADR} with fresh context. Emits findings by severity. Does not lock, edit, decide, or spawn. Invoke on design/audit stage. ([ZhevlakovII/aiworkflow](https://github.com/ZhevlakovII/aiworkflow/blob/HEAD/ports/claude/agents/critic.md) · 3 copies)
- **critic**: Independent critical pass over {source, spec, ADR} with fresh context. Emits findings by severity. Does not lock, edit, decide, or spawn. ([ZhevlakovII/aiworkflow](https://github.com/ZhevlakovII/aiworkflow/blob/HEAD/ports/dsh/agents/critic.md) · 2 copies)
- **Critic Agent**: Evaluates musical analyses for consistency, detects contradictions, scores response quality, and suggests improvements. ([GuitarAlchemist/ga](https://github.com/GuitarAlchemist/ga/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/critic.md) · ★2)
- **critic-affinity-matrix**: &gt; **NOTE:** The programmatic source of truth for critic selection is `pipeline/scripts/agent-config.json` with `pipeline/scripts/select-agents.sh`. This file is retained as human-readable documentation and fallback for when the script is unavailable. New domains SHOULD be added to agent-config.json first; update this file to keep it in sync. ([ydaromai/ai-sdlc](https://github.com/ydaromai/ai-sdlc/blob/HEAD/pipeline/agents/critic-affinity-matrix.md) · ★1)
- **critic-agent**: Use this agent for rigorous quality assessment of work produced by other agents, particularly producer-agent. Invoke after any significant artefact or deliverable is generated — and after each revision — to evaluate it against the task brief, applicable standards (such as writing-skills), and user requirements before presenting to the user. Produces a structured review with an accept-or-iterate decision. ([isvlasov/rageatc-oss](https://github.com/isvlasov/rageatc-oss/blob/HEAD/plugins/rageatc-core-oss/agents/critic-agent.md) · ★10)
- **critic-agent**: Polywave critic agent (E37) that reviews IMPL doc agent briefs against the actual codebase before wave execution. Reads every brief, reads every owned file, verifies accuracy across 6 checks, and writes a structured CriticResult to the IMPL doc. Runs after E16 validation, before REVIEWED state. Never modifies source files. ([blackwell-systems/polywave](https://github.com/blackwell-systems/polywave/blob/HEAD/implementations/claude-code/prompts/agents/critic-agent.md) · ★3)
- **critic-agent**: **Mindset:** You are NOT here to validate. You are here to find what is wrong, incomplete, risky, or fragile — before production does. ([zafrirron/Agent-Platform](https://github.com/zafrirron/Agent-Platform/blob/HEAD/AGENT-PLATFORM-TEMPLATES/.agent/agents/critic-agent.md) · ★3)
- **critic-agent**: Diğer ajanların çıktısını bağımsız olarak denetler. Mantık hatası, tutarsızlık, doğrulanmamış varsayım arar. Kod yazmaz, sadece değerlendirir. ([boranesn/agentic-base](https://github.com/boranesn/agentic-base/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-loop/agents/critic-agent.md) · ★2)
- **critic-agent**: Use this agent when the create-image skill needs to evaluate and rank generated images. This agent mirrors the Critic from the PaperBanana paper. ([noahrasheta/shipfast](https://github.com/noahrasheta/shipfast/blob/HEAD/create-image/agents/critic-agent.md) · ★1)
- **critic-agent**: 仕様書批評・検証の処理ガイドライン。 視点漏れ、矛盾、曖昧さ、実装困難性を指摘する手順を定義。 ※ このファイルは「実行者」ではなく「処理ガイドライン」です。 ※ メイン Claude Code がこのガイドラインを参照しながら直接実行します。 ([ishidafuu/isdf_padel](https://github.com/ishidafuu/isdf_padel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **critic-agent**: Use this agent to review and evaluate generated outputs for correctness, security, performance, and quality. The critic identifies issues but does not fix them. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: Generator has produced code implementation\nuser: "Review this authentication implementation for any issues"\nassistant: "I'll use the critic-agent to thoroughly review the authentication code for security, correctness, and quality issues."\n&lt;commentary&gt;\nThe critic will examine the code across multiple dimensions and provide structured feedback on any problems found.\n&lt;/commentary&gt;\n&lt;/example&gt;\n\n&lt;exa… ([jhershey/claude_agents](https://github.com/jhershey/claude_agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-agent.md))
- **critic-agent**: The plan compiled against the brief by a frontier model from another laboratory than the architect. Use in /plan, after the architect's plan is clean at the lint, before the human's approval. ([Marsouino/roblox_test](https://github.com/Marsouino/roblox_test/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-agent.md))
- **critic-agent**: PaperBanana-style 4D content critique: accuracy, conciseness, readability, actionability. Scores content on 4 dimensions with structured feedback and rewrite suggestions. ([zdenekmach/deep-research](https://github.com/zdenekmach/deep-research/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-agent.md))
- **critic-ai**: Adversarial reviewer of PM artifacts through the AI-product lens. Invoked only when a pm-os skill explicitly requests a critique of a named workspace file. Never for code review, never proactively. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-ai.md) · ★1)
- **critic-assassin**: Isolated product critic and market skeptic. Given a PRD/spec, returns a CriticalAnalysis JSON of failure modes, scale pain, confusion risks, engineering regrets, market-skeptic verdicts, and a kill recommendation. Dispatched by the reckoning skill; never run alongside the praise engine in a shared context. ([alchemyguy/reckoning](https://github.com/alchemyguy/reckoning/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-assassin.md))
- **critic-black-hat**: Adversarial critic. Finds concrete flaws, hidden costs, failure modes, and ethical risks in candidate ideas. De Bono's Black Hat applied as a per-round adversary. Does NOT discard ideas — surfaces weaknesses for evolution. ([giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent](https://github.com/giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-black-hat.md) · ★1)
- **critic-business**: Adversarial reviewer of PM artifacts through the business lens. Invoked only when a pm-os skill explicitly requests a critique of a named workspace file. Never for code review, never proactively. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-business.md) · ★1)
- **critic-clarity**: Adversarial reviewer of PM artifacts through the clarity-and-evidence lens. Invoked only when a pm-os skill explicitly requests a critique of a named workspace file. Never for code review, never proactively. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-clarity.md) · ★1)
- **critic-classification**: Adversarial audit of the retrospective's stage attributions and library health — verifies that each problem is attributed to its true upstream origin. Invoked in parallel with critic-coverage and critic-remediation at Submission. ([lacolaco/claude-plugins](https://github.com/lacolaco/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/retrospective/agents/critic-classification.md) · ★5)
- **critic-completeness**: The synthesiser — runs LAST in --depth deep, the one persona allowed to see the others' output. Finds what all reviewers missed, where they agreed too easily, and systemic issues that fall between mandates; flags findings it believes are wrong. May ADD findings and assign a triage `criticality` rank, but may NOT edit, downgrade, or delete another reviewer's severity. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-completeness.md))
- **critic-copy**: Read-only critic that reviews ad copy for clarity, brand voice, and truthfulness. Use to critique headlines, body, and CTAs before production. ([metinduraktr-44/adops-agents](https://github.com/metinduraktr-44/adops-agents/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-copy.md))
- **critic-coverage**: Adversarial audit of the retrospective for exhaustiveness — verifies that every rule and knowledge source was enumerated, all six stages were examined, and gap analysis is complete. Invoked in parallel with critic-classification and critic-remediation at Submission. ([lacolaco/claude-plugins](https://github.com/lacolaco/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/retrospective/agents/critic-coverage.md) · ★5)
- **critic-customer**: Adversarial reviewer of PM artifacts through the customer lens. Invoked only when a pm-os skill explicitly requests a critique of a named workspace file. Never for code review, never proactively. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-customer.md) · ★1)
- **critic-delivery**: Adversarial reviewer of PM artifacts through the delivery lens. Invoked only when a pm-os skill explicitly requests a critique of a named workspace file. Never for code review, never proactively. ([MehdiMhamdi/pm-os](https://github.com/MehdiMhamdi/pm-os/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-delivery.md) · ★1)
- **critic-design**: Read-only critic that reviews visual design for hierarchy, brand fit, and legibility. Use to critique layouts before export. ([metinduraktr-44/adops-agents](https://github.com/metinduraktr-44/adops-agents/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-design.md))
- **critic-goal-fit**: Plan-review critic (primary lens: goal-fit). Adversarially reviews a planner's ## Plan for whether it actually achieves the stated goal; also applies the state-fit lens. ([Osasuwu/jarvis](https://github.com/Osasuwu/jarvis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-goal-fit.md) · ★1)
- **critic-gtm**: Adversarial reviewer of PM artifacts through the go-to-market lens. Invoked only when a pm-os skill explicitly requests a critique of a named workspace file. - **critic-impl**: Adversarial read-only critic for `impl`-mode rounds — one instance per lens over code (a diff, a module, a script), which it reads but never executes. Spawned ONLY by the critic-ledger skill's orchestrator as stage "critic round"; it is never self-invoked, never invoked by the user directly, and never used outside a fix-ledger round. Do NOT auto-select this agent for any task: outside a critic-ledger round its constraints are meaningless and its output contract is wrong. ([Spoloborota/critic-ledger](https://github.com/Spoloborota/critic-ledger/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-impl.md))
- **critic-logic**: Adversarial Logic Critic — logic bugs, unhandled edge cases, happy-path assumptions, input-validation gaps, error-handling holes. Spawn in parallel with critic-security and critic-performance during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/critic-logic.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **critic-logic**: Review code for logic bugs, unhandled edge cases, happy-path assumptions, input-validation gaps, and error-handling holes. Spawn in parallel with critic-security and critic-performance during /vdd-multi. ([MatrixFounder/Agentic-development](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Agentic-development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-logic.md) · ★6)
- **critic-manager**: 검증 에이전트. 데이터 근거 충분성 + 문제-솔루션 연결성을 검증하고, 품질 미달 시 피드백과 개선 방향을 제시한다. PASS/REJECT 판정 후 다음 단계를 결정. ([khc-dev/khc-vc-po](https://github.com/khc-dev/khc-vc-po/blob/HEAD/pasta/agents/critic-manager.md))
- **critic-minority**: Voices minority/dissident perspectives that mainstream consensus would suppress. Different from black-hat — not about flaws, but about whose voice is missing. Inspired by "Amplifying Minority Voices" (arXiv 2502.06251). ([giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent](https://github.com/giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-minority.md) · ★1)
